// dist/admin-i18n/index.d.ts
import type { PluginTranslationSource } from "@trinacria-cms/kernel";
export type CorePackAdminLocale = "en" | "it";
export type CorePackAdminMessages = Readonly<Record<string, string>>;
/**
 * Canonical Backoffice dictionaries owned by Core Pack.
 *
 * They are referenced by the plugin manifest, persisted during provisioning, and
 * reused by the client only until the remote registry is available.
 */
export declare const CORE_PACK_ADMIN_I18N: Readonly<Record<CorePackAdminLocale, CorePackAdminMessages>>;
/** Package-local runtime assets; their message payload is never stored in the manifest. */
export declare const CORE_PACK_ADMIN_I18N_SOURCES: readonly PluginTranslationSource[];

// dist/infra/core-pack-mongo.module.d.ts
import { type Provider } from "@trinacria-cms/kernel";
import { createMongoDbAdapter, EntityRegistry } from "@trinacria-cms/kernel/runtime";
import { type ConnectOptions } from "mongoose";
export interface CorePackMongoModuleOptions {
    uri: string;
    options?: ConnectOptions;
    allowStartupWithoutDb?: boolean;
}
export declare const CORE_PACK_MONGO_OPTIONS_TOKEN: import("@trinacria/core").Token<CorePackMongoModuleOptions>;
export declare const CORE_PACK_MONGO_CONNECTION_TOKEN: import("@trinacria/core").Token<CorePackMongoConnection>;
/**
 * Manages mongoose connection lifecycle for core-pack infrastructure.
 */
declare class CorePackMongoConnection {
    private config;
    constructor(config: CorePackMongoModuleOptions);
    onInit(): Promise<void>;
    onDestroy(): Promise<void>;
    get connection(): Parameters<typeof createMongoDbAdapter>[0]["connection"];
    reconnect(uri: string, options?: ConnectOptions): Promise<void>;
    get configUri(): string;
}
/**
 * Factory for an infrastructure module that wires MongoDbAdapter + EntityRegistry.
 */
export declare function createCorePackMongoModule(options: CorePackMongoModuleOptions): Readonly<{
    name: string;
    providers: (import("@trinacria/core").ValueProvider<CorePackMongoModuleOptions> | import("@trinacria/core").ClassProvider<CorePackMongoConnection> | import("@trinacria/core").FactoryProvider<EntityRegistry> | import("@trinacria/core").FactoryProvider<import("@trinacria-cms/kernel").DbAdapter>)[];
    exports: (import("@trinacria/core").Token<import("@trinacria-cms/kernel").DbAdapter> | import("@trinacria/core").Token<EntityRegistry>)[];
}>;
/**
 * Factory for global providers that wire MongoDbAdapter + EntityRegistry.
 * Useful when plugin modules are loaded at runtime and need globally visible DB tokens.
 */
export declare function createCorePackMongoGlobalProviders(options: CorePackMongoModuleOptions): readonly Provider[];
export {};

// dist/infra/index.d.ts
export * from "./core-pack-mongo.module.js";

    id: string;
    email: string;
    status: "expired" | "revoked" | "available" | "consumed";
    createdAt: string;
    updatedAt: string;
    expiresAt: string;
    tokenHash: string;
    tokenType: "password_reset" | "email_verification" | "user_invite";

    userId?: string | undefined;
    consumedAt?: string | undefined;
}>;

    }>;

// dist/modules/auth/auth-session.d.ts
import { type HttpContext } from "@trinacria-cms/kernel";
import type { RuntimeConfigService } from "../settings/config/runtime-config.service.js";
import type { LoginResult } from "./services/auth.service.js";
export interface JwtCookieConfig {
    accessCookieName: string;
    refreshCookieName: string;
    path: string;
    domain?: string;
    sameSite: "Strict" | "Lax" | "None";
    secure: boolean;
}
export declare function readJwtCookieConfig(config: RuntimeConfigService, envFallback?: JwtCookieConfig): Promise<JwtCookieConfig>;
export declare function readJwtCookieConfigFromEnv(): JwtCookieConfig;
export declare function buildLoginSetCookieHeaders(session: LoginResult, config: JwtCookieConfig): readonly string[];
export declare function buildLogoutClearCookieHeaders(config: JwtCookieConfig): readonly string[];
export declare function extractAccessTokenFromCookie(ctx: Pick<HttpContext, "req">, config: JwtCookieConfig): string | null;
export declare function extractRefreshTokenFromCookie(ctx: Pick<HttpContext, "req">, config: JwtCookieConfig): string | null;

// dist/modules/auth/auth.controller.d.ts
import { HttpController } from "@trinacria-cms/kernel";
import type { JwtAuthService } from "./services/auth.service.js";
import type { AuthUserFlowsService } from "./services/auth-user-flows.service.js";
/**
 * Public authentication API for local admin/operator login.
 */
export declare class AuthController extends HttpController {
    private readonly auth;
    private readonly flows;
    private readonly authMiddleware;
    constructor(auth: JwtAuthService, flows: AuthUserFlowsService);
    routes(): import("@trinacria/http").RouteDefinition[];
    private login;
    private completeMfaLogin;
    private beginLoginMfaEnrollment;
    private completeLoginMfaEnrollment;
    private mfaStatus;
    private beginMfaEnrollment;
    private confirmMfaEnrollment;
    private disableMfa;
    private respondWithSession;
    private me;
    private requestPasswordReset;
    private registerPublic;
    private acceptUserInvite;
    private completePasswordReset;
    private requestEmailVerification;
    private confirmEmailVerification;
    private updateMe;
    private changePassword;
    private logout;
}

// dist/modules/auth/auth.middleware.d.ts
import { type HttpContext, type HttpMiddleware } from "@trinacria-cms/kernel";
import { type UserRecord } from "../users/users.schemas.js";
import { type JwtAuthService } from "./services/auth.service.js";
export declare const AUTHENTICATED_USER_STATE_KEY = "corePack.auth.authenticatedUser";
/**
 * Bearer-token middleware for JWT authentication.
 * On success, the authenticated user is attached to `ctx.state`.
 */
export declare function createJwtAuthMiddleware(auth: JwtAuthService, options?: {
    requireAdmin?: boolean;
}): HttpMiddleware;
/**
 * Reads authenticated user from request state.
 */
export declare function getAuthenticatedUser(ctx: HttpContext): UserRecord;
/**
 * Extracts bearer token from Authorization header.
 */
export declare function extractBearerToken(ctx: HttpContext): string | null;
/**
 * Extracts JWT token from Authorization bearer header or access-token cookie.
 * Header takes precedence over cookie when both are present.
 */
export declare function extractAuthToken(ctx: HttpContext, cookieConfig?: import("./auth-session.js").JwtCookieConfig): string | null;

// dist/modules/auth/services/auth-mfa.service.d.ts
import type { UserRecord } from "../../users/users.schemas.js";
import type { AuthMfaChallengePurpose } from "../auth-mfa.schemas.js";
import type { AuthMfaRepository } from "../repositories/auth-mfa.repository.js";
export declare class AuthMfaService {
    private readonly repository;
    constructor(repository: AuthMfaRepository);
    hasEnabledFactor(userId: string): Promise<boolean>;
    getStatus(userId: string, mode: MfaMode): Promise<{
        mode: MfaMode;
        enabled: boolean;
        enabledAt?: string;
        recoveryCodesRemaining: number;
    }>;
    beginEnrollment(user: UserRecord): Promise<EnrollmentSetup>;
    confirmEnrollment(userId: string, code: string): Promise<{
        recoveryCodes: readonly string[];
    }>;
    verifyCode(userId: string, code: string): Promise<boolean>;
    disable(userId: string): Promise<void>;
    createChallenge(userId: string, purpose: AuthMfaChallengePurpose): Promise<MfaChallenge>;
    resolveChallenge(challengeId: string, expectedPurpose: AuthMfaChallengePurpose): Promise<{
        id: string;
        userId: string;
    }>;
    consumeChallenge(id: string): Promise<void>;
}
export type MfaMode = "disabled" | "optional" | "required";
export interface MfaChallenge {
    challengeId: string;
    purpose: AuthMfaChallengePurpose;
    expiresAt: string;
}
export interface EnrollmentSetup {
    manualKey: string;
    otpauthUrl: string;
    expiresAt: string;
}

import type { EventBus } from "@trinacria/events";

    constructor(users: AuthUsersRepository, localCredentials: LocalCredentialsRepository, passwordHashing: PasswordHashingService, tokens: AuthFlowTokensRepository, config: RuntimeConfigService, securePayloads: SecureEventPayloadClient, events: Pick<EventBus, "emit">, atomic?: (<T>(work: (service: AuthUserFlowsService) => Promise<T>) => Promise<T>) | undefined);
    requestPasswordReset(email: string): Promise<{
        accepted: true;
    }>;
    completePasswordReset(input: {
        token: string;
        newPassword: string;
    }): Promise<{
        completed: true;
    }>;

        accepted: true;
    }>;

        completed: true;

        accepted: true;
    }>;

    private getNumber;

// dist/modules/auth/services/auth.service.d.ts
import type { DbAdapter } from "@trinacria-cms/kernel";
import type { InstallationStateRepository } from "../../installation/repositories/installation-state.repository.js";
import type { LocalCredentialsRepository } from "../../installation/repositories/local-credentials.repository.js";
import type { PasswordHashingService } from "../../installation/services/password-hashing.service.js";
import type { RuntimeConfigService } from "../../settings/config/runtime-config.service.js";
import type { UserRecord } from "../../users/users.schemas.js";
import { type JwtCookieConfig } from "../auth-session.js";
import type { AuthBlacklistRepository } from "../repositories/auth-blacklist.repository.js";
import type { AuthLoginAttemptRepository } from "../repositories/auth-login-attempt.repository.js";
import type { AuthUsersRepository } from "../repositories/auth-users.repository.js";
import type { AuthMfaService, MfaMode } from "./auth-mfa.service.js";
export interface LoginResult {
    accessToken: string;
    refreshToken: string;
    tokenType: "Bearer";
    expiresAt: string;
    refreshExpiresAt: string;
    user: UserRecord;
}
export interface MfaLoginChallengeResult {
    status: "mfa_required" | "mfa_enrollment_required";
    challengeId: string;
    expiresAt: string;
}
export type PasswordLoginResult = LoginResult | MfaLoginChallengeResult;
/**
 * Typed authentication error used by JWT auth API and middleware.
 */
export declare class JwtAuthError extends Error {
    readonly code: string;
    readonly details?: Record<string, unknown>;
    constructor(code: string, message: string, details?: Record<string, unknown>);
}
/**
 * Service handling login and JWT validation.
 *
 * Token revocation and brute-force tracking are persisted to MongoDB.
 * Survives restarts and is shared across replicas.
 */
export declare class JwtAuthService {
    private readonly users;
    private readonly localCredentials;
    private readonly installationState;
    private readonly passwordHashing;
    private readonly blacklist;
    private readonly loginAttempts;
    private readonly config;
    private readonly db;
    private readonly mfa?;
    private authSettingsCache?;
    private cookieConfigCache?;
    constructor(users: AuthUsersRepository, localCredentials: LocalCredentialsRepository, installationState: InstallationStateRepository, passwordHashing: PasswordHashingService, blacklist: AuthBlacklistRepository, loginAttempts: AuthLoginAttemptRepository, config: RuntimeConfigService, db: DbAdapter, mfa?: AuthMfaService | undefined);
    loginWithPassword(input: {
        email: string;
        password: string;
    }): Promise<LoginResult>;
    /**
     * HTTP login entry point. Password verification always happens first; a session
     * is issued only after the configured MFA policy has been satisfied.
     */
    beginPasswordLogin(input: {
        email: string;
        password: string;
    }): Promise<PasswordLoginResult>;
    completeMfaLogin(challengeId: string, code: string): Promise<LoginResult>;
    beginMfaEnrollmentForLogin(challengeId: string): Promise<{
        manualKey: string;
        otpauthUrl: string;
        expiresAt: string;
    }>;
    completeMfaEnrollmentForLogin(challengeId: string, code: string): Promise<LoginResult & {
        recoveryCodes: readonly string[];
    }>;
    getMfaStatus(userId: string): Promise<{
        mode: MfaMode;
        enabled: boolean;
        enabledAt?: string;
        recoveryCodesRemaining: number;
    }>;
    beginMfaEnrollment(userId: string): Promise<{
        manualKey: string;
        otpauthUrl: string;
        expiresAt: string;
    }>;
    confirmMfaEnrollment(userId: string, code: string): Promise<{
        recoveryCodes: readonly string[];
    }>;
    disableMfa(userId: string, input: {
        currentPassword: string;
        code: string;
    }): Promise<void>;
    private getMfaService;
    private authenticatePassword;
    private issueSession;
    authenticateBearerToken(token: string, options?: {
        requireAdmin?: boolean;
    }): Promise<UserRecord>;
    revokeBearerToken(token: string): Promise<boolean>;
    authenticateRefreshToken(token: string): Promise<UserRecord>;
    updateAuthenticatedUserProfile(userId: string, input: {
        firstName: string;
        lastName: string;
        locale?: "en" | "it";
    }): Promise<UserRecord>;
    changeAuthenticatedUserPassword(userId: string, input: {
        currentPassword: string;
        newPassword: string;
    }): Promise<UserRecord>;
    getJwtCookieConfig(): Promise<JwtCookieConfig>;
    private assertNotLockedOut;
    private recordFailedAttempt;
    private assertTokenNotRevoked;
    private assertInstallationCompleted;
    private getAuthConfig;
    /** MFA policy changes must take effect on the next password login, not after the auth cache TTL. */
    private getMfaMode;
}

    delNamespace(namespace: string): Promise<void>;

// dist/modules/cache/services/cache.service.d.ts
import type { CacheAdapter } from "../adapters/cache-adapter.js";
/**
 * Namespace-aware cache service with automatic loading via getOrCompute.
 *
 * Every value is stored under a namespace to allow targeted invalidation
 * (e.g., invalidate all "permissions" entries without touching "roles").
 */
export declare class CacheService {
    private readonly adapter;
    constructor(adapter: CacheAdapter);
    get<T>(namespace: string, key: string): Promise<T | undefined>;
    set<T>(namespace: string, key: string, value: T, ttlSeconds?: number): Promise<void>;
    getOrCompute<T>(namespace: string, key: string, loader: () => Promise<T>, ttlSeconds?: number): Promise<T>;
    invalidate(namespace: string, key?: string): Promise<void>;
    clear(): Promise<void>;
    /**
     * Wraps a loader function with caching, returning a function
     * that checks the cache first before calling the original loader.
     */
    wrap<T>(namespace: string, key: string, loader: () => Promise<T>, ttlSeconds?: number): () => Promise<T>;
}

// dist/modules/i18n/i18n-messages.repository.d.ts
import type { DbAdapter } from "@trinacria-cms/kernel";
import { type TranslationMessageRecord } from "./i18n-messages.schemas.js";
export declare class I18nMessagesRepository {
    private readonly db;
    private scope?;
    constructor(db: DbAdapter);
    upsert(input: Omit<TranslationMessageRecord, "id" | "createdAt" | "updatedAt">): Promise<{
        id: string;
        locale: string;
        createdAt: string;
        updatedAt: string;
        key: string;
        value: string;
        pluginId: string;
        source: string;
        namespace: string;
        surface: string;
        sourceVersion: string;
    } & {}>;
    listByPlugin(pluginId: string): Promise<readonly TranslationMessageRecord[]>;
    listByLocale(locale: string, namespace?: string, surface?: string): Promise<readonly TranslationMessageRecord[]>;
    deleteById(id: string): Promise<boolean>;
    private repository;
}

// dist/modules/i18n/i18n-messages.schemas.d.ts
import { type Infer } from "@trinacria-cms/kernel";
/** One small, independently updatable message imported from a plugin package asset. */
export declare const TranslationMessageRecordSchema: import("@trinacria/schema/dist/core").InternalSchema<{
    id: string;
    locale: string;
    createdAt: string;
    updatedAt: string;
    key: string;
    value: string;
    pluginId: string;
    source: string;
    namespace: string;
    surface: string;
    sourceVersion: string;
} & {}>;
export type TranslationMessageRecord = Infer<typeof TranslationMessageRecordSchema>;
/** Central registry of granular plugin translations. */
export declare const I18N_MESSAGES_ENTITY: {
    ownerPluginId: string;
    entityName: string;
    schema: import("@trinacria/schema/dist/core").InternalSchema<{
        id: string;
        locale: string;
        createdAt: string;
        updatedAt: string;
        key: string;
        value: string;
        pluginId: string;
        source: string;
        namespace: string;
        surface: string;
        sourceVersion: string;
    } & {}>;
    indexes?: readonly import("@trinacria-cms/kernel/runtime").EntityIndexDefinition[];
};

// dist/modules/i18n/i18n-messages.service.d.ts
import type { PluginManifest, PluginTranslationSource } from "@trinacria-cms/kernel";
import type { CacheService } from "../cache/services/cache.service.js";
import type { I18nMessagesRepository } from "./i18n-messages.repository.js";
/** Imports package assets into granular message records and resolves client dictionaries. */
export declare class I18nMessagesService {
    private readonly messages;
    private readonly cache?;
    constructor(messages: I18nMessagesRepository, cache?: CacheService | undefined);
    syncManifest(manifest: PluginManifest, sources?: readonly PluginTranslationSource[]): Promise<void>;
    removePlugin(pluginId: string): Promise<void>;
    /** Resolves English first and overlays the requested locale with a single indexed collection. */
    resolveLocale(locale: string, namespace?: string, surface?: string): Promise<{
        locale: string;
        fallbackLocale: "en";
        namespace?: string;
        messages: Readonly<Record<string, string>>;
    }>;
    private resolveManifestMessages;
}

/**

    email: string;
    firstName: string;
    lastName: string;
    siteName: string;
    password: string;
    confirmPassword: string;

    siteTagline?: string | undefined;
    timezone?: string | undefined;
}>;

    envFilePresent: boolean;
    dbConfigured: boolean;
    envFilePath: string;
} & {
    installedAt?: string | undefined;
    adminUserId?: string | undefined;
}>;
/**
 * OpenAPI response schema for installation status endpoint.
 */
export declare const InstallationStatusResponseSchema: import("@trinacria/schema/dist/core/schema.js").InternalSchema<{
    data: {
        installed: boolean;
        envFilePresent: boolean;
        dbConfigured: boolean;
        envFilePath: string;
    } & {
        installedAt?: string | undefined;
        adminUserId?: string | undefined;
    };
} & {
    meta?: ({} & {
        pluginId?: "core-pack" | undefined;
    }) | undefined;
}>;
/**
 * OpenAPI response schema for successful bootstrap endpoint.
 */
export declare const InstallationBootstrapResponseSchema: import("@trinacria/schema/dist/core/schema.js").InternalSchema<{
    data: {
        status: {
            installed: boolean;
            envFilePresent: boolean;
            dbConfigured: boolean;
            envFilePath: string;
        } & {
            installedAt?: string | undefined;
            adminUserId?: string | undefined;
        };
        adminUser: {
            id: string;
            email: string;
            firstName: string;
            lastName: string;
            status: "active" | "suspended";
            createdAt: string;
            updatedAt: string;
        } & {
            locale?: "en" | "it" | undefined;
        };
    } & {};
} & {
    meta?: ({} & {
        pluginId?: "core-pack" | undefined;
    }) | undefined;
}>;
export declare const InstallationErrorResponseSchema: import("@trinacria/schema/dist/core/schema.js").InternalSchema<{
    error: {
        code: string;
        message: string;
    } & {};
} & {
    meta?: ({} & {
        pluginId?: "core-pack" | undefined;
    }) | undefined;
}>;

// dist/modules/installation/installation.schemas.d.ts
import { type Infer } from "@trinacria-cms/kernel";
export declare const INSTALLATION_STATE_KEY: "core-pack";
export declare const PasswordAlgorithmSchema: import("@trinacria/schema/dist/core/schema.js").InternalSchema<"scrypt-v1">;
/**
 * Singleton installation state for core-pack bootstrap flow.
 */
export declare const InstallationStateRecordSchema: import("@trinacria/schema/dist/core/schema.js").InternalSchema<{
    id: string;
    createdAt: string;
    updatedAt: string;
    kind: "install_state";
    key: "core-pack";
    installed: boolean;
} & {
    installedAt?: string | undefined;
    adminUserId?: string | undefined;
}>;
export type InstallationStateRecord = Infer<typeof InstallationStateRecordSchema>;
/**
 * Local credentials record storing only password hash material.
 */
export declare const LocalCredentialRecordSchema: import("@trinacria/schema/dist/core/schema.js").InternalSchema<{
    id: string;
    createdAt: string;
    updatedAt: string;
    userId: string;
    algorithm: "scrypt-v1";
    passwordHash: string;
    passwordSalt: string;
    passwordUpdatedAt: string;
} & {}>;
export type LocalCredentialRecord = Infer<typeof LocalCredentialRecordSchema>;
export declare const LOCAL_CREDENTIALS_ENTITY: {
    ownerPluginId: string;
    entityName: string;
    schema: import("@trinacria/schema/dist/core/schema.js").InternalSchema<{
        id: string;
        createdAt: string;
        updatedAt: string;
        userId: string;
        algorithm: "scrypt-v1";
        passwordHash: string;
        passwordSalt: string;
        passwordUpdatedAt: string;
    } & {}>;
    indexes?: readonly import("@trinacria-cms/kernel/runtime").EntityIndexDefinition[];
};

// dist/modules/installation/offline-installer.module.d.ts
import { type DbAdapter, HttpController } from "@trinacria-cms/kernel";
/** Only environmental status is mounted while Mongo is unavailable. No business plugin is executed. */
export declare class OfflineInstallerController extends HttpController {
    private readonly db;
    constructor(db: DbAdapter);
    routes(): import("@trinacria/http").RouteDefinition[];
}
export declare const CorePackOfflineInstallerModule: Readonly<{
    name: string;
    providers: import("@trinacria/core").ClassProvider<HttpController>[];

// dist/modules/installation/services/installation.service.d.ts
import type { PluginManifestProvisioner } from "@trinacria-cms/kernel";
import type { MongoDurableEventStore } from "@trinacria-cms/kernel/runtime";
import type { UserAccessService } from "../../security/user-access/user-access.service.js";
import type { SettingsService } from "../../settings/services/settings.service.js";
import { UsersRepository } from "../../users/repositories/users.repository.js";
import type { UserRecord } from "../../users/users.schemas.js";
import type { InstallBootstrapInput } from "../dto/installation.input.dto.js";
import type { InstallationStateRepository } from "../repositories/installation-state.repository.js";
import type { LocalCredentialsRepository } from "../repositories/local-credentials.repository.js";
import type { PasswordHashingService } from "./password-hashing.service.js";
export interface InstallationStatus {
    installed: boolean;
    installedAt?: string;
    adminUserId?: string;
    envFilePresent: boolean;
    dbConfigured: boolean;
    envFilePath: string;
}
export interface InstallationBootstrapResult {
    status: InstallationStatus;
    adminUser: UserRecord;
}
/**
 * Typed error used when bootstrap is requested after installation completion.
 */
export declare class InstallationAlreadyCompletedError extends Error {
    readonly code: "installation_already_completed";
    constructor();
}
export declare class PasswordMismatchError extends Error {
    readonly code: "password_mismatch";
    constructor();
}
/**
 * Coordinates the one-time CMS bootstrap flow:
 * - ensure security baseline exists
 * - create/reactivate admin user
 * - store local credentials hash
 * - attach admin role
 * - save site settings
 * - persist installation state
 *
 * MongoDB is expected to be pre-configured via .env at application startup.
 */
export declare class InstallationService {
    private readonly installationState;
    private readonly localCredentials;
    private readonly users;
    private readonly userAccess;
    private readonly manifestProvisioning;
    private readonly passwordHashing;
    private readonly settings;
    private readonly durable?;
    constructor(installationState: InstallationStateRepository, localCredentials: LocalCredentialsRepository, users: UsersRepository, userAccess: UserAccessService, manifestProvisioning: PluginManifestProvisioner, passwordHashing: PasswordHashingService, settings: SettingsService, durable?: MongoDurableEventStore | undefined);
    getStatus(): Promise<InstallationStatus>;
    bootstrap(input: InstallBootstrapInput): Promise<InstallationBootstrapResult>;
    private provisionSiteSettings;
    private upsertAdminUser;
    private writeAdminUser;
    private toStatus;
}

// dist/modules/installation/services/password-hashing.service.d.ts
export interface PasswordHashResult {
    algorithm: "scrypt-v1";
    passwordHash: string;
    passwordSalt: string;
}
/**
 * Encapsulates password hashing and verification logic for local credentials.
 * The service is intentionally algorithm-versioned to support future migrations.
 */
export declare class PasswordHashingService {
    hashPassword(password: string): Promise<PasswordHashResult>;
    verifyPassword(password: string, credential: {
        algorithm: "scrypt-v1";
        passwordHash: string;
        passwordSalt: string;
    }): Promise<boolean>;
}

    }): Promise<PermissionRecord>;

}

    private readonly db;
    private scope?;

    upsert(input: UpsertRoleGrantInput): Promise<RoleGrantRecord>;

    listBySourcePlugin(sourcePluginId: string): Promise<readonly RoleGrantRecord[]>;
    deleteById(id: string): Promise<boolean>;
    deleteBySourcePlugin(sourcePluginId: string): Promise<number>;
    deleteByRoleCode(roleCode: string): Promise<number>;
    hasGrantFromOtherPlugins(roleCode: string, excludedPluginId: string): Promise<boolean>;
    private repository;
    private findRoleByCode;
    private listAllRoles;
    private parseRoleDocument;
    private toRecord;
    private buildGrantId;
    private toPermissionKeys;
}

// dist/modules/roles/grants/role-grants.schemas.d.ts
import { type Infer } from "@trinacria-cms/kernel";
/**
 * Embedded grant payload stored inside a role document.
 * It keeps contribution ownership for safe plugin deprovisioning.
 */
export declare const EmbeddedRoleGrantSchema: import("@trinacria/schema/dist/core").InternalSchema<{
    createdAt: string;
    updatedAt: string;
    sourcePluginId: string;
    permissionKey: string;
} & {}>;
export type EmbeddedRoleGrant = Infer<typeof EmbeddedRoleGrantSchema>;
/**
 * API-facing record linking one role to one permission.
 * It is derived from embedded grants and keeps backward-compatible shape.
 */
export declare const RoleGrantRecordSchema: import("@trinacria/schema/dist/core").InternalSchema<{
    id: string;
    createdAt: string;
    updatedAt: string;
    sourcePluginId: string;
    permissionKey: string;
    roleCode: string;
} & {}>;
export type RoleGrantRecord = Infer<typeof RoleGrantRecordSchema>;

// dist/modules/roles/repositories/roles.repository.d.ts
import type { DbAdapter } from "@trinacria-cms/kernel";
import type { CacheService } from "../../cache/services/cache.service.js";
import { type CreateRoleInput, type UpdateRoleInput, type UpdateRoleStatusInput } from "../dto/roles.input.dto.js";
import { type RoleRecord } from "../roles.schemas.js";
/**
 * Persistence adapter for roles module over kernel DbAdapter.
 */
export declare class RolesRepository {
    private readonly db;
    private readonly cache?;
    private scope?;
    constructor(db: DbAdapter, cache?: CacheService | undefined);
    create(input: CreateRoleInput): Promise<RoleRecord>;
    findById(id: string): Promise<RoleRecord | null>;
    private findByIdFromDb;
    findByCode(code: string): Promise<RoleRecord | null>;
    private findByCodeFromDb;
    list(options?: {
        limit?: number;
        offset?: number;
    }): Promise<readonly RoleRecord[]>;
    listByCodes(codes: readonly string[]): Promise<readonly RoleRecord[]>;
    listOwnedByPlugin(pluginId: string): Promise<readonly RoleRecord[]>;
    updateStatus(id: string, input: UpdateRoleStatusInput): Promise<RoleRecord | null>;
    updateDetails(id: string, input: UpdateRoleInput): Promise<RoleRecord | null>;
    upsertOwnedRole(input: {
        code: string;
        name: string;
        description?: string;
        ownerPluginId: string;
    }): Promise<RoleRecord>;
    deleteById(id: string): Promise<boolean>;
    /**
     * Low-level update used by the provisioning service for embedded array operations.
     */
    findRawById(id: string): Promise<Record<string, unknown> | null>;
    rawUpdate(id: string, patch: Record<string, unknown>): Promise<void>;
    rawCompareAndSwap(id: string, expectedUpdatedAt: string, patch: Record<string, unknown>): Promise<boolean>;
    private repository;
    private parseRoleRecord;
}

// dist/modules/roles/roles.controller.d.ts

    private readonly adminAuthMiddleware;
    constructor(roles: RolesOperations, auth: JwtAuthService);
    routes(): import("@trinacria/http").RouteDefinition[];
    private listRoles;
    private getRoleById;
    private createRole;
    private updateRole;
    private updateRoleStatus;
}

// dist/modules/roles/roles.schemas.d.ts
import { type Infer } from "@trinacria-cms/kernel";
export declare const RoleStatusSchema: import("@trinacria/schema/dist/core").InternalSchema<"active" | "disabled">;
export declare const RolePermissionKeySchema: import("@trinacria/schema").Schema<string>;
export declare const RolePolicyRuleEffectSchema: import("@trinacria/schema/dist/core").InternalSchema<"allow" | "deny">;
export declare const RolePolicyRuleConditionSchema: import("@trinacria/schema/dist/core").InternalSchema<"resource_id_required" | "resource_id_equals_subject">;
export declare const EmbeddedRolePolicyRuleSchema: import("@trinacria/schema/dist/core").InternalSchema<{
    createdAt: string;
    updatedAt: string;
    sourcePluginId: string;
    effect: "allow" | "deny";
    permissionPattern: string;
    conditions: ("resource_id_required" | "resource_id_equals_subject")[];
} & {}>;
export type EmbeddedRolePolicyRule = Infer<typeof EmbeddedRolePolicyRuleSchema>;
export declare const RoleRecordSchema: import("@trinacria/schema/dist/core").InternalSchema<{
    id: string;
    status: "active" | "disabled";
    createdAt: string;
    updatedAt: string;
    code: string;
    name: string;
} & {
    ownerPluginId?: string | undefined;
    permissions?: string[] | undefined;
    description?: string | undefined;
    policyRules?: ({
        createdAt: string;
        updatedAt: string;
        sourcePluginId: string;
        effect: "allow" | "deny";
        permissionPattern: string;
        conditions: ("resource_id_required" | "resource_id_equals_subject")[];
    } & {})[] | undefined;
}>;
export type RoleRecord = Infer<typeof RoleRecordSchema>;
/**
 * Canonical roles entity declaration.
 * It defines schema and logical indexes in one place.
 */
export declare const ROLES_ENTITY: {
    ownerPluginId: string;
    entityName: string;
    schema: import("@trinacria/schema/dist/core").InternalSchema<{
        id: string;
        status: "active" | "disabled";
        createdAt: string;
        updatedAt: string;
        code: string;
        name: string;
    } & {
        ownerPluginId?: string | undefined;
        permissions?: string[] | undefined;
        description?: string | undefined;
        policyRules?: ({
            createdAt: string;
            updatedAt: string;
            sourcePluginId: string;
            effect: "allow" | "deny";
            permissionPattern: string;
            conditions: ("resource_id_required" | "resource_id_equals_subject")[];
        } & {})[] | undefined;
    }>;
    indexes?: readonly import("@trinacria-cms/kernel/runtime").EntityIndexDefinition[];
};

// dist/modules/security/audit/security-audit.d.ts
import { type AuditEntry, type AuditSink, type DbAdapter } from "@trinacria-cms/kernel";
export declare const SECURITY_AUDIT_ENTITY: {
    ownerPluginId: string;
    entityName: string;
    schema: import("@trinacria/schema/dist/core").InternalSchema<{} & {}>;
    indexes?: readonly import("@trinacria-cms/kernel/runtime").EntityIndexDefinition[];
};
export declare class SecurityAuditStore implements AuditSink {
    private readonly db;
    private readonly retentionDays;
    private readonly includePlatform;
    constructor(db: DbAdapter, retentionDays?: number, includePlatform?: boolean);
    append(input: Omit<AuditEntry, "id" | "timestamp">): Promise<void>;
    appendIn(db: DbAdapter, input: Omit<AuditEntry, "id" | "timestamp">): Promise<void>;
    list(input?: {
        ownerPluginId?: string;
        actorId?: string;
        since?: string;
        until?: string;
        limit?: number;
        offset?: number;
    }): Promise<readonly AuditEntry[]>;
}

// dist/modules/security/policies/authz-rules.d.ts
/**
 * Common authorization rule shape shared by user-based and API-key-based
 * subjects before evaluation inside the AuthzService.
 */
export interface AuthorizationRule {
    effect: "allow" | "deny";
    permissionPattern: string;
    conditions: readonly ("resource_id_required" | "resource_id_equals_subject")[];
}
/**
 * Dedupe helper so multiple sources (direct grants, roles, policy rules)
 * collapse into a stable set of authorization rules.
 */
export declare function dedupeAuthorizationRules(rules: readonly AuthorizationRule[]): readonly AuthorizationRule[];

// dist/modules/security/role-policy-rules/role-policy-rules.repository.d.ts
import type { DbAdapter } from "@trinacria-cms/kernel";
import { type RolePolicyRuleRecord } from "./role-policy-rules.schemas.js";
export interface UpsertRolePolicyRuleInput {
    roleCode: string;
    effect: "allow" | "deny";
    permissionPattern: string;
    conditions?: readonly ("resource_id_required" | "resource_id_equals_subject")[];
    sourcePluginId: string;
}
/**
 * Persistence adapter for role policy rules backed by embedded arrays in `roles`.
 * No separate collection is used — rules are stored inside the role document.
 */
export declare class RolePolicyRulesRepository {
    private readonly db;
    private scope?;
    constructor(db: DbAdapter);
    upsert(input: UpsertRolePolicyRuleInput): Promise<RolePolicyRuleRecord>;
    findById(id: string): Promise<RolePolicyRuleRecord | null>;
    findOne(input: UpsertRolePolicyRuleInput): Promise<RolePolicyRuleRecord | null>;
    listByRoleCodes(roleCodes: readonly string[]): Promise<readonly RolePolicyRuleRecord[]>;
    listByRoleCode(roleCode: string): Promise<readonly RolePolicyRuleRecord[]>;
    listBySourcePlugin(sourcePluginId: string): Promise<readonly RolePolicyRuleRecord[]>;
    deleteById(id: string): Promise<boolean>;
    deleteBySourcePlugin(sourcePluginId: string): Promise<number>;
    updateById(id: string, patch: {
        effect: "allow" | "deny";
        permissionPattern: string;
        conditions: readonly ("resource_id_required" | "resource_id_equals_subject")[];
        updatedAt: string;
    }): Promise<RolePolicyRuleRecord | null>;
    private normalizeInput;
    private toRecord;
    private buildRuleId;
    private parseRuleId;
    private findRoleByCode;
    private parseRoleDocument;
    private repository;
}

// dist/modules/security/role-policy-rules/role-policy-rules.schemas.d.ts
import { type Infer } from "@trinacria-cms/kernel";

export { EmbeddedRolePolicyRuleSchema, RolePolicyRuleConditionSchema, RolePolicyRuleEffectSchema };
/**
 * API-facing record for policy rules returned by REST endpoints.
 * Derived from the embedded array inside a role document.
 */
export declare const RolePolicyRuleRecordSchema: import("@trinacria/schema/dist/core/schema.js").InternalSchema<{
    id: string;
    createdAt: string;
    updatedAt: string;
    sourcePluginId: string;
    effect: "allow" | "deny";
    permissionPattern: string;
    conditions: ("resource_id_required" | "resource_id_equals_subject")[];
    roleCode: string;
} & {}>;
export type RolePolicyRuleRecord = Infer<typeof RolePolicyRuleRecordSchema>;

// dist/modules/security/role-policy-rules/role-policy-rules.service.d.ts
import type { RolesRepository } from "../../roles/repositories/roles.repository.js";
import type { RolePolicyRulesRepository } from "./role-policy-rules.repository.js";
/**
 * Application service for role policy rule CRUD APIs.
 */
export declare class RolePolicyRulesService {
    private readonly roles;
    private readonly rules;
    constructor(roles: RolesRepository, rules: RolePolicyRulesRepository);
    listByRoleCode(roleCode: string): Promise<readonly ({
        id: string;
        createdAt: string;
        updatedAt: string;
        sourcePluginId: string;
        effect: "allow" | "deny";
        permissionPattern: string;
        conditions: ("resource_id_required" | "resource_id_equals_subject")[];
        roleCode: string;
    } & {})[] | null>;
    create(roleCode: string, input: {
        effect: "allow" | "deny";
        permissionPattern: string;
        conditions?: readonly ("resource_id_required" | "resource_id_equals_subject")[];
    }): Promise<({
        id: string;
        createdAt: string;
        updatedAt: string;
        sourcePluginId: string;
        effect: "allow" | "deny";
        permissionPattern: string;
        conditions: ("resource_id_required" | "resource_id_equals_subject")[];
        roleCode: string;
    } & {}) | null>;
    update(roleCode: string, id: string, input: {
        effect: "allow" | "deny";
        permissionPattern: string;
        conditions?: readonly ("resource_id_required" | "resource_id_equals_subject")[];
    }): Promise<({
        id: string;
        createdAt: string;
        updatedAt: string;
        sourcePluginId: string;
        effect: "allow" | "deny";
        permissionPattern: string;
        conditions: ("resource_id_required" | "resource_id_equals_subject")[];
        roleCode: string;
    } & {}) | null>;
    delete(roleCode: string, id: string): Promise<boolean | null>;
}

// dist/modules/security/services/security-provisioning.service.d.ts
import type { PluginManifest, PluginManifestProvisioner, PluginTranslationSource } from "@trinacria-cms/kernel";
import type { I18nMessagesService } from "../../i18n/i18n-messages.service.js";

import type { UserRolesRepository } from "../user-access/user-roles.repository.js";
/**
 * Materializes manifest-owned Core resources: security, settings, and i18n.
 * Ownership is tracked by `sourcePluginId` to keep uninstall operations safe.
 *
 * Policy rules are stored as an embedded array inside the role document
 * (no separate collection is used).
 */
export declare class CorePackManifestProvisioningService implements PluginManifestProvisioner {
    private readonly roles;
    private readonly roleGrants;
    private readonly permissions;
    private readonly userRoles;
    private readonly settings;
    private readonly i18nMessages?;
    private static readonly POLICY_UPDATE_MAX_RETRIES;
    private readonly deferredManifests;
    constructor(roles: RolesRepository, roleGrants: RoleGrantsRepository, permissions: PermissionsRepository, userRoles: UserRolesRepository, settings: SettingsService, i18nMessages?: I18nMessagesService | undefined);
    provision(manifest: PluginManifest, i18nSources?: readonly PluginTranslationSource[]): Promise<void>;
    defer(manifest: PluginManifest): void;
    provisionDeferred(): Promise<void>;
    deprovision(manifest: PluginManifest): Promise<void>;
    private syncOwnedGrants;
    private syncOwnedPermissions;
    private upsertEmbeddedPolicyRule;
    private listRolesPolicyRules;
    private deletePolicyRulesBySourcePlugin;
    private syncOwnedPolicyRules;
    private syncOwnedRoles;
    private serializeGrant;
    private normalizeSecurity;
    private syncManifestSettings;
    private updateRolePolicyRulesWithRetry;
}

}

 * Runtime guard validating a value as JSON-compatible payload.
 */

 * Validates and returns a normalized JSON-compatible payload.
 */
export declare function parseJsonValue(value: unknown): JsonValue;
/**
 * Performs a deep clone preserving JSON semantics.
 */
export declare function cloneJsonValue<T extends JsonValue>(value: T): T;

import type { DbAdapter } from "@trinacria-cms/kernel";
type Logger = {
    info(message: string, metadata?: Record<string, unknown>): void;
    warn(message: string, metadata?: Record<string, unknown>): void;
    error(message: string, metadata?: Record<string, unknown>): void;
};
export interface RuntimeConfigGetStringOptions {
    fallback?: string;
    envVar?: string;
}
export interface RuntimeConfigGetNumberOptions {
    fallback?: number;
    min?: number;
    max?: number;
    envVar?: string;
}
export interface RuntimeConfigGetBooleanOptions {
    fallback?: boolean;
    envVar?: string;
}
export interface RuntimeConfigGetJsonOptions<T = unknown> {
    fallback?: T;
    envVar?: string;
}
export declare class RuntimeConfigService {
    private readonly db;
    private readonly logger?;
    private cache;
    private strictMode;
    constructor(db: DbAdapter, logger?: Logger | undefined);
    setStrictMode(enabled: boolean): void;
    getString(key: string, opts?: RuntimeConfigGetStringOptions): Promise<string | undefined>;
    getNumber(key: string, opts?: RuntimeConfigGetNumberOptions): Promise<number | undefined>;
    getBoolean(key: string, opts?: RuntimeConfigGetBooleanOptions): Promise<boolean | undefined>;
    getJson<T = unknown>(key: string, opts?: RuntimeConfigGetJsonOptions<T>): Promise<T | undefined>;
    invalidate(key?: string): void;
    private getFromCache;
    private setCache;
    private readFromDb;
    private readFromEnv;
    private clampNumber;
    private warnEnvFallback;
}
export {};

import { type DbAdapter, type PluginAccessGrant, type PluginAccessGrantStatus } from "@trinacria-cms/kernel";

export interface PluginGrantIdentity {
    producerPluginId: string;
    consumerPluginId: string;
    accessType: NonNullable<PluginAccessGrant["accessType"]>;
    resource?: string;
    action?: string;
    operation?: string;
    requiredPermission: string;
    workspaceId?: string;
}
export interface PluginGrantRecord extends PluginAccessGrant {
    id: string;
    accessType: NonNullable<PluginAccessGrant["accessType"]>;
    revision: number;
    target: string;
    payloadType: string;
    workspaceId: string;
    updatedAt: string;
    revokedAt?: string;
}

    ownerPluginId: string;
    entityName: string;
    schema: import("@trinacria/schema/dist/core/schema.js").InternalSchema<{} & {}>;
    indexes?: readonly import("@trinacria-cms/kernel/runtime").EntityIndexDefinition[];
};
export declare class PluginGrantConflictError extends Error {
    readonly code = "plugin_grant_conflict";
    readonly status = 409;
}
export declare function normalizeGrant(input: PluginGrantIdentity): Omit<PluginGrantRecord, "status" | "revision" | "updatedAt">;
export declare class PluginGrantsRepository {
    private readonly db;
    private readonly audit;
    private readonly instanceId;
    private initialization?;
    constructor(db: DbAdapter, audit?: SecurityAuditStore, instanceId?: string);
    private repository;
    private ready;

    list(limit?: number, offset?: number): Promise<readonly PluginGrantRecord[]>;
    find(input: PluginGrantIdentity): Promise<PluginGrantRecord | null>;
    request(input: PluginGrantIdentity): Promise<PluginGrantRecord>;
    decide(id: string, status: Exclude<PluginAccessGrantStatus, "pending">, expectedRevision: number, actorId: string, reason: string): Promise<PluginGrantRecord>;
    private transaction;
}

// dist/modules/settings/plugin-access/trusted-plugin-access-policy.service.d.ts
import type { OperationContext, OperationTarget, PluginAccessAuthorizationResult, PluginEventSubscriptionAuthorizationRequest, PluginOperationAuthorizationRequest, PluginRuntime, SecureEventPayloadAuthorizationRequest } from "@trinacria-cms/kernel/contracts";

// dist/modules/settings/schemas/settings.schemas.d.ts
import { type Infer, type Schema } from "@trinacria-cms/kernel";
import { type JsonValue } from "../_shared/settings-json.js";
export declare const SettingDefinitionStatusSchema: import("@trinacria/schema/dist/core/schema.js").InternalSchema<"active" | "disabled">;
export declare const SettingVisibilitySchema: import("@trinacria/schema/dist/core/schema.js").InternalSchema<"admin" | "public" | "internal">;
export declare const SettingRecordKindSchema: import("@trinacria/schema/dist/core/schema.js").InternalSchema<"value" | "definition" | "install_state" | "blacklist" | "login_attempt" | "secret">;
/**
 * Schema that accepts any JSON-compatible value (object, array, primitive, null).
 * MongoDB stores these natively as nested documents — no string serialization needed.
 */
export declare function jsonValue<T extends JsonValue = JsonValue>(): Schema<T>;
/**
 * Unified settings storage shape.
 * `kind` discriminates public settings views and core-pack internal records.
 *
 * JSON sub-objects (schema, defaultValue, value) are stored directly as
 * native MongoDB documents rather than serialized strings.
 */
export declare const SettingRecordSchema: import("@trinacria/schema/dist/core/schema.js").InternalSchema<{
    id: string;
    createdAt: string;
    kind: "value" | "definition" | "install_state" | "blacklist" | "login_attempt" | "secret";
    key: string;
} & {
    email?: string | undefined;
    status?: "active" | "disabled" | undefined;
    updatedAt?: string | undefined;
    value?: JsonValue | undefined;
    defaultValue?: JsonValue | undefined;
    installed?: boolean | undefined;
    installedAt?: string | undefined;
    adminUserId?: string | undefined;
    algorithm?: "aes-256-gcm" | undefined;
    sub?: string | undefined;
    iat?: number | undefined;
    tokenKind?: "access" | "refresh" | undefined;
    expiresAt?: string | undefined;
    count?: number | undefined;
    lockoutUntil?: string | null | undefined;
    secret?: boolean | undefined;
    ownerPluginId?: string | undefined;
    description?: string | undefined;
    category?: string | undefined;
    schema?: JsonValue | undefined;
    visibility?: "admin" | "public" | "internal" | undefined;
    mutable?: boolean | undefined;
    version?: number | undefined;
    cipherText?: string | undefined;
    iv?: string | undefined;
    authTag?: string | undefined;
    keyVersion?: string | undefined;
    updatedBy?: string | undefined;
}>;
export type SettingRecord = Infer<typeof SettingRecordSchema>;
/**
 * Logical projection for definition records in unified settings collection.
 */
export declare const SettingDefinitionRecordSchema: import("@trinacria/schema/dist/core/schema.js").InternalSchema<{
    id: string;
    status: "active" | "disabled";
    createdAt: string;
    updatedAt: string;
    kind: "definition";
    key: string;
    secret: boolean;
    ownerPluginId: string;
    visibility: "admin" | "public" | "internal";
    mutable: boolean;
} & {
    defaultValue?: JsonValue | undefined;
    description?: string | undefined;
    category?: string | undefined;
    schema?: JsonValue | undefined;
}>;
export type SettingDefinitionRecord = Infer<typeof SettingDefinitionRecordSchema>;
/**
 * Logical projection for explicit value records in unified settings collection.
 */
export declare const SettingValueRecordSchema: import("@trinacria/schema/dist/core/schema.js").InternalSchema<{
    id: string;
    createdAt: string;
    updatedAt: string;
    kind: "value";
    key: string;
    value: JsonValue;
    ownerPluginId: string;
    version: number;
} & {
    updatedBy?: string | undefined;
}>;
export type SettingValueRecord = Infer<typeof SettingValueRecordSchema>;
/**
 * Logical projection for encrypted secret records in unified settings collection.
 */
export declare const SettingSecretRecordSchema: import("@trinacria/schema/dist/core/schema.js").InternalSchema<{
    id: string;
    createdAt: string;
    updatedAt: string;
    kind: "secret";
    key: string;
    algorithm: "aes-256-gcm";
    ownerPluginId: string;
    cipherText: string;
    iv: string;
    authTag: string;
    keyVersion: string;
} & {
    updatedBy?: string | undefined;
}>;
export type SettingSecretRecord = Infer<typeof SettingSecretRecordSchema>;
/**
 * Unified settings entity declaration.
 * All settings records are stored in this single collection.
 */
export declare const SETTINGS_ENTITY: {
    ownerPluginId: string;
    entityName: string;
    schema: import("@trinacria/schema/dist/core/schema.js").InternalSchema<{
        id: string;
        createdAt: string;
        kind: "value" | "definition" | "install_state" | "blacklist" | "login_attempt" | "secret";
        key: string;
    } & {
        email?: string | undefined;
        status?: "active" | "disabled" | undefined;
        updatedAt?: string | undefined;
        value?: JsonValue | undefined;
        defaultValue?: JsonValue | undefined;
        installed?: boolean | undefined;
        installedAt?: string | undefined;
        adminUserId?: string | undefined;
        algorithm?: "aes-256-gcm" | undefined;
        sub?: string | undefined;
        iat?: number | undefined;
        tokenKind?: "access" | "refresh" | undefined;
        expiresAt?: string | undefined;
        count?: number | undefined;
        lockoutUntil?: string | null | undefined;
        secret?: boolean | undefined;
        ownerPluginId?: string | undefined;
        description?: string | undefined;
        category?: string | undefined;
        schema?: JsonValue | undefined;
        visibility?: "admin" | "public" | "internal" | undefined;
        mutable?: boolean | undefined;
        version?: number | undefined;
        cipherText?: string | undefined;
        iv?: string | undefined;
        authTag?: string | undefined;
        keyVersion?: string | undefined;
        updatedBy?: string | undefined;
    }>;
    indexes?: readonly import("@trinacria-cms/kernel/runtime").EntityIndexDefinition[];
};

// dist/modules/settings/secrets/settings-secrets-crypto.service.d.ts
import type { RuntimeConfigService } from "../config/runtime-config.service.js";
export interface SettingsSecretsCryptoOptions {
    masterKey?: string;
    keyVersion?: string;
    strictMasterKeyRequired?: boolean;
    config?: RuntimeConfigService;
}
export interface EncryptedSecretPayload {
    cipherText: string;
    iv: string;
    authTag: string;
    algorithm: "aes-256-gcm";
    keyVersion: string;
}
/**
 * AES-256-GCM helper for settings secrets encryption.
 * This service is intentionally small and deterministic for easy auditing.
 */
export declare class SettingsSecretsCryptoService {
    private readonly algorithm;
    private readonly keyVersion;
    private readonly key;
    private readonly strictMasterKeyRequired;
    constructor(options?: SettingsSecretsCryptoOptions);
    private readonly config;
    encrypt(plaintext: string): EncryptedSecretPayload;
    decrypt(payload: {
        cipherText: string;
        iv: string;
        authTag: string;
    }): string;
    private resolveKey;
    static createFromConfig(config: RuntimeConfigService, masterKey?: string): Promise<SettingsSecretsCryptoService>;
}

// dist/modules/settings/services/settings.service.d.ts
import { type JsonValue } from "../_shared/settings-json.js";
import type { SettingsDefinitionsRepository } from "../definitions/settings-definitions.repository.js";
import type { SettingsSecretsRepository } from "../secrets/settings-secrets.repository.js";
import type { SettingsSecretsCryptoService } from "../secrets/settings-secrets-crypto.service.js";
import type { SettingsValuesRepository } from "../values/settings-values.repository.js";
export interface SettingsDefinition {
    id: string;
    key: string;
    ownerPluginId: string;
    category?: string;
    description?: string;
    schema?: JsonValue;
    defaultValue?: JsonValue;
    visibility: "public" | "admin" | "internal";
    mutable: boolean;
    secret: boolean;
    status: "active" | "disabled";
    createdAt: string;
    updatedAt: string;
}
export interface SettingValue {
    id: string;
    key: string;
    ownerPluginId: string;
    value: JsonValue;
    version: number;
    updatedBy?: string;
    createdAt: string;
    updatedAt: string;
}
export interface SettingSecretMetadata {
    id: string;
    key: string;
    ownerPluginId: string;
    algorithm: "aes-256-gcm";
    keyVersion: string;
    maskedValue: string;
    updatedBy?: string;
    createdAt: string;
    updatedAt: string;
}
export interface ResolvedSettingValue {
    key: string;
    ownerPluginId: string;
    value: JsonValue;
    source: "value" | "default";
    version?: number;
    updatedAt: string;
}
export interface SettingsObservabilitySnapshot {
    reads: number;
    writes: number;
    denies: number;
    errors: number;
}
export interface ExportedPluginSettings {
    pluginId: string;
    definitions: readonly SettingsDefinition[];
    values: readonly SettingValue[];
    secrets: readonly SettingSecretMetadata[];
}
export interface SettingsGroupSummary {
    id: string;
    label: string;
    ownerPluginIds: readonly string[];
    definitionCount: number;
    editableCount: number;
    secretCount: number;
}
export interface SettingsGroupField {
    fieldId: string;
    key: string;
    ownerPluginId: string;
    domain: string;
    name: string;
    definition: SettingsDefinition;
    value?: JsonValue;
    source?: "value" | "default";
    version?: number;
    updatedAt?: string;
    secretMetadata?: SettingSecretMetadata;
}
export interface SettingsGroupSnapshot {
    id: string;
    label: string;
    ownerPluginIds: readonly string[];
    values: Record<string, JsonValue>;
    fields: readonly SettingsGroupField[];
}
export interface SettingsGroupUpdateResult {
    group: SettingsGroupSnapshot;
    updated: readonly SettingValue[];
}
/**
 * Application service for settings definitions, values and encrypted secrets.
 */
export declare class SettingsService {
    private readonly definitions;
    private readonly values;
    private readonly secrets;
    private readonly crypto;
    private readonly metrics;
    constructor(definitions: SettingsDefinitionsRepository, values: SettingsValuesRepository, secrets: SettingsSecretsRepository, crypto: SettingsSecretsCryptoService);
    /** Host-only session rebinding; callers still use the owner-bound host capability. */
    forRepositories(definitions: SettingsDefinitionsRepository, values: SettingsValuesRepository, secrets: SettingsSecretsRepository): SettingsService;
    upsertDefinition(input: {
        requesterPluginId: string;
        key: string;
        category?: string;
        description?: string;
        schema?: unknown;
        defaultValue?: unknown;
        visibility?: "public" | "admin" | "internal";
        mutable?: boolean;
        secret?: boolean;
        status?: "active" | "disabled";
    }): Promise<SettingsDefinition>;
    getDefinitionByKey(key: string): Promise<SettingsDefinition | null>;
    /** Permanently removes a retired setting and every persisted representation of it. */
    deleteRetiredSetting(input: {
        requesterPluginId: string;
        key: string;
    }): Promise<void>;
    listDefinitions(options?: {
        ownerPluginId?: string;
        limit?: number;
        offset?: number;
    }): Promise<readonly SettingsDefinition[]>;
    upsertValue(input: {
        requesterPluginId: string;
        key: string;
        value: unknown;
        updatedBy?: string;
    }): Promise<SettingValue>;
    getResolvedValueByKey(key: string): Promise<ResolvedSettingValue | null>;
    getResolvedValueForPlugin(requesterPluginId: string, key: string): Promise<ResolvedSettingValue | null>;
    listDefinitionsForPlugin(requesterPluginId: string, options?: {
        ownerPluginId?: string;
        limit?: number;
        offset?: number;
    }): Promise<readonly SettingsDefinition[]>;
    getDefinitionByKeyForPlugin(requesterPluginId: string, key: string): Promise<SettingsDefinition | null>;
    listGroups(options?: {
        ownerPluginId?: string;
    }): Promise<readonly SettingsGroupSummary[]>;
    listGroupsForPlugin(requesterPluginId: string): Promise<readonly SettingsGroupSummary[]>;
    getGroupById(groupId: string, options?: {
        ownerPluginId?: string;
    }): Promise<SettingsGroupSnapshot | null>;
    getGroupForPlugin(requesterPluginId: string, groupId: string): Promise<SettingsGroupSnapshot | null>;
    upsertGroupValues(input: {
        requesterPluginId: string;
        groupId: string;
        values: Record<string, unknown>;
        updatedBy?: string;
        admin?: boolean;
    }): Promise<SettingsGroupUpdateResult>;
    upsertSecret(input: {
        requesterPluginId: string;
        key: string;
        plaintext: string;
        updatedBy?: string;
    }): Promise<SettingSecretMetadata>;
    getSecretMetadata(requesterPluginId: string, key: string): Promise<SettingSecretMetadata | null>;
    getSecretMetadataByKey(key: string): Promise<SettingSecretMetadata | null>;
    revealSecret(requesterPluginId: string, key: string): Promise<{
        key: string;
        value: string;
    } | null>;
    exportPluginSettings(requesterPluginId: string, pluginId: string): Promise<ExportedPluginSettings>;
    getObservabilitySnapshot(): SettingsObservabilitySnapshot;
    private listActiveDefinitions;
    private toGroupSummaries;
    private toGroupSnapshot;
    private toDefinition;
    private toSettingValue;
    private toSecretMetadata;
    private getActiveDefinitionForWrite;
    private trackFailure;
}

// dist/modules/users/events/user-events.catalog.d.ts
import type { PluginManifestEmittedEvent } from "@trinacria-cms/kernel/contracts";
import type { UserRecord } from "../users.schemas.js";
export declare const CORE_PACK_USER_CREATED_EVENT: string;
export declare const CORE_PACK_USER_PROFILE_UPDATED_EVENT: string;
export declare const CORE_PACK_USER_STATUS_CHANGED_EVENT: string;
export declare const CORE_PACK_USER_INVITED_EVENT: string;
export declare const CORE_PACK_USER_INVITE_ACCEPTED_EVENT: string;
export type CorePackUserEventSource = "admin" | "public-registration" | "system";
export interface CorePackUserCreatedPayload {
    userId: string;
    status: UserRecord["status"];
    source: CorePackUserEventSource;
}
export interface CorePackUserProfileUpdatedPayload {
    userId: string;
    changedFields: readonly ("firstName" | "lastName")[];
}
export interface CorePackUserStatusChangedPayload {
    userId: string;
    previousStatus: UserRecord["status"];
    status: UserRecord["status"];
    reason?: "admin" | "email-verification" | "invite-accepted" | "system";
}
export interface CorePackUserInvitedPayload {
    userId: string;
    actorUserId?: string;
}
export interface CorePackUserInviteAcceptedPayload {
    userId: string;
}
export declare const CORE_PACK_USER_EVENT_DEFINITIONS: readonly PluginManifestEmittedEvent[];

        id: string;

        lastName: string;

};

export declare function createUserAccessOperations(service: UserAccessService, authorizer: OperationAuthorizer): UserAccessOperations;

// dist/operations/core-operation-authorizer.d.ts
import type { AuditSink, AuthzService, OperationAuthorizer, OperationContext, OperationTarget } from "@trinacria-cms/kernel/contracts";
/** Installed-plugin contracts and external HTTP access decisions. */
export interface PluginApplicationGrantPolicy {
    canOperate(context: OperationContext, target: OperationTarget): Promise<{
        allowed: boolean;
    }>;
}
export declare class CoreOperationAuthorizer implements OperationAuthorizer {
    private readonly authz;
    private readonly pluginGrants?;
    private readonly audit?;
    constructor(authz: AuthzService, pluginGrants?: PluginApplicationGrantPolicy | undefined, audit?: AuditSink | undefined);
    readonly metrics: {
        denials: number;
        auditFailures: number;
    };
    run<T>(context: OperationContext, targets: readonly OperationTarget[], work: () => Promise<T>): Promise<T>;
    assert(context: OperationContext, target: OperationTarget): Promise<void>;
    private check;
}

// dist/operations/plugin-grant-operations.d.ts

export type PluginGrantOperations = ReturnType<typeof createPluginGrantOperations>;
export declare const PLUGIN_GRANT_OPERATIONS: import("@trinacria/core").Token<import("@trinacria-cms/kernel").ApplicationOperations<Pick<PluginGrantsRepository, "list" | "get" | "decide">>>;
export declare function createPluginGrantOperations(repository: PluginGrantsRepository, authorizer: OperationAuthorizer): import("@trinacria-cms/kernel").ApplicationOperations<Pick<PluginGrantsRepository, "list" | "get" | "decide">>;

 * Canonical identifier for the core-pack plugin.
 * Shared across manifest, modules, and API metadata.
 */

/**
 * Source identifier used for policy rules created manually via core-pack HTTP APIs.
 * It is intentionally different from `CORE_PACK_PLUGIN_ID` so manifest sync
 * does not overwrite runtime/admin-defined rules.
 */
export declare const CORE_PACK_MANUAL_POLICY_SOURCE: "core-pack-manual";

// dist/plugin/core-pack.security.d.ts
/**
 * Canonical capability catalog exposed by core-pack.
 * Keep this as single source of truth to avoid string drift.
 */
export declare const CORE_PACK_CAPABILITIES: {
    readonly PLUGINS_READ: "plugins.read";
    readonly USERS_READ: "users.read";
    readonly USERS_WRITE: "users.write";
    readonly ROLES_READ: "roles.read";
    readonly ROLES_WRITE: "roles.write";
    readonly PERMISSIONS_READ: "permissions.read";
    readonly PERMISSIONS_WRITE: "permissions.write";
    readonly SETTINGS_READ: "settings.read";
    readonly SETTINGS_WRITE: "settings.write";
    readonly SETTINGS_SECRETS_READ: "settings.secrets.read";
    readonly SETTINGS_SECRETS_WRITE: "settings.secrets.write";
};
export type CorePackCapability = (typeof CORE_PACK_CAPABILITIES)[keyof typeof CORE_PACK_CAPABILITIES];
export declare const CORE_PACK_CAPABILITY_LIST: readonly CorePackCapability[];
/**
 * Canonical permission keys exposed by core-pack.
 * They are namespaced through `CORE_PACK_PLUGIN_ID`.
 */
export declare const CORE_PACK_PERMISSION_KEYS: {
    readonly PLUGINS_MANAGE: string;
    readonly PLUGIN_GRANTS_READ: string;
    readonly PLUGIN_GRANTS_MANAGE: string;
    readonly MIGRATIONS_READ: string;
    readonly MIGRATIONS_APPLY: string;
    readonly DELIVERIES_READ: string;
    readonly DELIVERIES_MANAGE: string;
    readonly EMAIL_JOBS_READ: string;
    readonly EMAIL_JOBS_MANAGE: string;
    readonly AUDIT_READ: string;
    readonly PLUGINS_READ: string;
    readonly USERS_READ: string;
    readonly USERS_WRITE: string;
    readonly ROLES_READ: string;
    readonly ROLES_WRITE: string;
    readonly PERMISSIONS_READ: string;
    readonly PERMISSIONS_WRITE: string;
    readonly SETTINGS_READ: string;
    readonly SETTINGS_WRITE: string;
    readonly SETTINGS_SECRETS_READ: string;
    readonly SETTINGS_SECRETS_WRITE: string;
};
export type CorePackPermissionKey = (typeof CORE_PACK_PERMISSION_KEYS)[keyof typeof CORE_PACK_PERMISSION_KEYS];
export interface CorePackPermissionDefinition {
    key: CorePackPermissionKey;
    displayName: string;
    description?: string;
}
/**
 * Public permission definition list used by the core-pack manifest and reusable
 * by other packages at compile-time.
 */
export declare const CORE_PACK_PERMISSION_DEFINITIONS: readonly CorePackPermissionDefinition[];
export declare const CORE_PACK_PERMISSION_KEY_LIST: readonly CorePackPermissionKey[];
/**
 * Default role metadata exported for consistency across integrations.
 */
export declare const CORE_PACK_ADMIN_ROLE: Readonly<import("@trinacria-cms/kernel").PluginManifestSecurityRole>;
export declare const CORE_PACK_EDITOR_ROLE: Readonly<import("@trinacria-cms/kernel").PluginManifestSecurityRole>;
export declare const CORE_PACK_VIEWER_ROLE: Readonly<import("@trinacria-cms/kernel").PluginManifestSecurityRole>;
export declare const CORE_PACK_DEFAULT_ROLES: readonly Readonly<import("@trinacria-cms/kernel").PluginManifestSecurityRole>[];
export declare const CORE_PACK_READONLY_PERMISSION_KEY_LIST: readonly CorePackPermissionKey[];
